## 1. Executive Summary

Overall risk: **Undetermined**. The pipeline recorded 20 unrated, conditional design threats: six STRIDE, three PASTA, one LINDDUN, three attack-tree goals and seven FMEA failure modes. They are complementary perspectives on shared specifications, not 20 independently demonstrated vulnerabilities. No implementation exists, and no control effectiveness or residual-risk assessment is available.

The supported concerns involve project execution authority, evidence integrity, private-data disclosure, report interpretation, revision/publication binding, restoration, uncertain dependency usage, API failure handling and availability. No supplied rating method supports a severity ranking. Recommendations retain model order in section 7; prioritization, owners, effort and deadlines are not supplied. The model preserves 98 exact first-release requirements, but implementation satisfaction is not assessed.

**Baseline and later decisions:** This report and immutable Appendix A assess commit `32a2bb4f2d46915a51a424ec6610a979d55d6b24`. Subsequent decisions in [SECURITY.md](SECURITY.md), [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md), [REQUIREMENTS.md](REQUIREMENTS.md) and [DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md) respond to these threats. They are specified, unverified decisions—not evidence that a baseline gap has been fixed in code. Section 7 distinguishes those decisions from the original action register. No original conflict, gap, rating or control status was retroactively overwritten.

## 2. Scope and Methodology

### 2.1 System Description

Trim Reaper reduces third-party dependencies while preserving application behavior and runtime compatibility. Operators run a non-interactive CLI against GitHub.com repositories; maintainers independently review verified changes.

### 2.2 Scope

All four complete specifications, CLI policy/coordinator, project execution, GitHub integration, package-source access, proposed intelligence API and terminal/Markdown reporting are included. No web/mobile application, separate product accounts, confirmed AI lifecycle, external model provider or implementation is inferred. Stage 06 excludes .git internals and treats logo.svg as artwork, not application code.

### 2.6 Limitations

All threat scenarios depend on unverified control failure or conditional data content. Repeated analysis of the same four specifications does not provide independent corroboration. Source hashes identify the baseline. No incident history, execution test, operating deployment, approved risk scale or quantitative impact is supplied. T-010 additionally depends on personal data being present. Stage07 flow details and AI-F1 have one analyzer observation and unresolved endpoint/granularity reconciliation (U-G002). No legal applicability or compliance conclusion is made. Figure briefs have not been rendered.

## 4. Threat Inventory

Method groups retain model order. Every rating is Unrated; no cross-method ranking is valid.

<a id="t-001"></a>

### T-001: Project execution reaches operator authority

**Rating:** Unrated. **Rating method:** Not supplied.
**Category:** STRIDE / Elevation of Privilege.
**Affected Assets:** STRIDE-a1 (Operator environment and GitHub credential access), STRIDE-a2 (Selected revision, isolated working copies and project artifacts), STRIDE-b1 (Local control to untrusted project execution), STRIDE-e2 (Repository code, wrappers, package contents and execution output), STRIDE-f2 (Execute supplied checks and declared toolchains).
**Trust Boundary:** STRIDE-b1.
**Evidence:** STRIDE-E1, STRIDE-E2, STRIDE-E3, STRIDE-E4.

#### Description

Declared wrappers, installation/build/test/runtime commands execute repository/package code. If execution crosses its intended boundary it could access operator files or GitHub credential authority; isolated copies alone do not contain execution.

#### Attack Scenario

Documented input reaches the affected responsibility → conditional failure of the specified boundary/control → outcome described above; feasibility not established. Conditional design path only; no exploit or execution is claimed. Assumptions: Cross-boundary access is conditional; no executable implementation exists.

#### Business Impact

Unknown: no quantified or approved business-impact assessment supplied.

#### Existing Controls

STRIDE-C8: Project command and filesystem isolation — BLOCKED; STRIDE-C10: GitHub credential handling and publication recovery — BLOCKED. No effectiveness results supplied.

#### Recommended Mitigations

Apply attached planned controls: Project command and filesystem isolation; GitHub credential handling and publication recovery. Concrete implementation/effectiveness evidence not supplied. Owner: Not supplied.

#### Residual Risk

Not assessed. Related perspectives: T-007, T-011, T-017. These share baseline evidence, not independent corroboration.

<a id="t-002"></a>

### T-002: Untrusted observations substitute for policy

**Rating:** Unrated. **Rating method:** Not supplied.
**Category:** STRIDE / Tampering.
**Affected Assets:** STRIDE-a3 (Coordinator, policy core and immutable verification evidence), STRIDE-a5 (Intelligence API and CLI adapter; contract unresolved), STRIDE-b5 (Untrusted observations to trusted policy and report presentation), STRIDE-f3 (Project/tool observations feed policy), STRIDE-f4 (Proposed agreed analysis inputs and returned suggestions).
**Trust Boundary:** STRIDE-b5, STRIDE-b2.
**Evidence:** STRIDE-E1, STRIDE-E2, STRIDE-E3, STRIDE-E4.

#### Description

Project/tool/API observations could be treated as authority to bypass verification, remove an uncertain dependency or publish an unverified change. Existing policy ownership explicitly forbids this.

#### Attack Scenario

Documented input reaches the affected responsibility → conditional failure of the specified boundary/control → outcome described above; feasibility not established. Conditional design path only; no exploit or execution is claimed. Assumptions: No additional assumption recorded beyond conditional control failure.

#### Business Impact

Unknown: no quantified or approved business-impact assessment supplied.

#### Existing Controls

STRIDE-C1: Keep untrusted evidence out of control decisions — specified_ACTIVE_not_implemented; STRIDE-C3: Preserve verification failure gates — specified_ACTIVE_not_implemented; STRIDE-C4: Preserve sensitive library implementations — specified_ACTIVE_not_implemented. No effectiveness results supplied.

#### Recommended Mitigations

Apply attached planned controls: Keep untrusted evidence out of control decisions; Preserve verification failure gates; Preserve sensitive library implementations. Concrete implementation/effectiveness evidence not supplied. Owner: Not supplied.

#### Residual Risk

Not assessed. Related perspectives: T-005, T-008, T-012, T-015, T-016, T-018, T-019, T-020. These share baseline evidence, not independent corroboration.

### T-007: Untrusted execution compromises run environment

**Rating:** Unrated. **Rating method:** Not supplied.
**Category:** PASTA / Execution authority.
**Affected Assets:** STRIDE-a1 (Operator environment and GitHub credential access), STRIDE-a2 (Selected revision, isolated working copies and project artifacts), STRIDE-a3 (Coordinator, policy core and immutable verification evidence), STRIDE-f2 (Execute supplied checks and declared toolchains), STRIDE-b1 (Local control to untrusted project execution).

Declared wrapper/package/project code enters verification execution (document claim). → It obtains authority beyond intended execution scope (conditional assumption). → Operator environment or credential access is affected (conditional consequence).. Conditional design path only; no exploit or execution is claimed. Assumptions: Execution containment failure is conditional.

#### Business Impact

Unknown: no quantified or approved business-impact assessment supplied.

#### Existing Controls

STRIDE-C8: Project command and filesystem isolation — BLOCKED; STRIDE-C10: GitHub credential handling and publication recovery — BLOCKED. No effectiveness results supplied.

#### Recommended Mitigations

Apply attached planned controls: Project command and filesystem isolation; GitHub credential handling and publication recovery. Concrete implementation/effectiveness evidence not supplied. Owner: Not supplied.

### T-008: Evidence-to-publication integrity failure

**Rating:** Unrated. **Rating method:** Not supplied.
**Category:** PASTA / Integrity.
**Affected Assets:** STRIDE-a2 (Selected revision, isolated working copies and project artifacts), STRIDE-a3 (Coordinator, policy core and immutable verification evidence), STRIDE-a4 (Terminal, Markdown report and review-only pull request), STRIDE-f3 (Project/tool observations feed policy), STRIDE-f5 (Verified final revision/report published using GitHub adapter), STRIDE-b5 (Untrusted observations to trusted policy and report presentation).
**Trust Boundary:** STRIDE-b5, STRIDE-b3.
**Evidence:** PASTA-E1, PASTA-E2, PASTA-E3, PASTA-E4.

#### Description

Untrusted observations or later state changes could sever the link between accepted candidate, verified final revision and published PR.

#### Attack Scenario

Project/tool/API observations enter local core (document claim). → Observation bypasses policy or final state changes after verification (conditional assumption). → GitHub adapter publishes an unverified or mismatched revision/report (conditional consequence).. Conditional design path only; no exploit or execution is claimed. Assumptions: No observed mutation race or policy bypass.

#### Business Impact

Unknown: no quantified or approved business-impact assessment supplied.

#### Existing Controls

STRIDE-C1: Keep untrusted evidence out of control decisions — specified_ACTIVE_not_implemented; STRIDE-C2: Enforce repository and publication restrictions — specified_ACTIVE_not_implemented; STRIDE-C3: Preserve verification failure gates — specified_ACTIVE_not_implemented; STRIDE-C10: GitHub credential handling and publication recovery — BLOCKED. No effectiveness results supplied.

#### Recommended Mitigations

Apply attached planned controls: Keep untrusted evidence out of control decisions; Enforce repository and publication restrictions; Preserve verification failure gates; GitHub credential handling and publication recovery. Concrete implementation/effectiveness evidence not supplied. Owner: Not supplied.

#### Residual Risk

Not assessed. Related perspectives: T-002, T-005, T-012, T-015, T-016, T-018, T-019, T-020. These share baseline evidence, not independent corroboration.

**Affected Assets:** AT-A3 (Report integrity).
**Trust Boundary:** Not explicitly mapped at this threat granularity; see affected records and related threats.
**Evidence:** AT-E1, AT-E2, AT-E3, AT-E4.

#### Description

Misrepresent results through rendered untrusted content

#### Attack Scenario

Untrusted names or command output reach reports → Renderer interprets those bytes as terminal controls or Markdown structure. Conditional design path only; no exploit or execution is claimed. Assumptions: Adversarial control of supplied project content or output; success depends on a control failure not evidenced by implementation.

#### Business Impact

Unknown: no quantified or approved business-impact assessment supplied.

#### Existing Controls

AT-C3: Safe terminal and Markdown representation unresolved — BLOCKED specification decision. No effectiveness results supplied.

#### Recommended Mitigations

Apply attached planned controls: Safe terminal and Markdown representation unresolved. Concrete implementation/effectiveness evidence not supplied. Owner: Not supplied.

#### Residual Risk

Not assessed. Related perspectives: T-004, T-014. These share baseline evidence, not independent corroboration.

### 6.2 Gap Analysis

No demonstrated implementation defect is established. Missing controls/effectiveness evidence is distinct from the documented blocked design decisions. All 50 provenance-bearing gap records remain in Appendix A, including repeated observations; U-G001–U-G006 summarize needed implementation tests, detailed interfaces, component identities, authoritative decisions, privacy/AI applicability and boundary tests.

| Conflict ID | Preserved baseline disagreement |
| --- | --- |
| STRIDE-CF1 | Runtime/framework labels are reversed in supplied developer input; proposed conventional mapping is not confirmed. |
| PASTA-CF1 | Runtime/framework labels are reversed in supplied developer input; proposed conventional mapping is not confirmed. |
| LINDDUN-CF1 | Runtime/framework labels are reversed in supplied developer input; proposed conventional mapping is not confirmed. |
| AT-X1 | Exact untruncated names/results versus rendering and disclosure protections has no resolved representation in input documents. |
| FM-X1 | Exact reporting and safe rendering/disclosure are not reconciled in input decisions. |
| S07-CF001 | Architecture records supplied Node.js/framework and Fastify/runtime labels versus proposed conventional mapping. Preserve both; AQ-1 and OD-001 unresolved. |
| S07-CF002 | SECURITY.md records direct Node.js 26 candidate versus Fastify parent Node.js 24/TypeScript 7. Neither candidate establishes runtime selection or compatibility. |
| S07-CF003 | DESIGN requires exact names and untruncated evidence; SECURITY OD-006/OD-008 asks how escaping/redaction preserves evidence without exposing secrets or active terminal/Markdown content. This is a design constraint requiring explicit reconciliation, not a demonstrated flaw. |

### 6.3 Defense in Depth Review

The architecture assigns candidate acceptance to the core, lifecycle/publication eligibility to the coordinator and credentialed publication to the GitHub adapter. These are documented enforcement responsibilities, not independent runtime defenses. Execution containment, evidence immutability, renderer safety and API disclosure still lack effectiveness evidence. Cross-method repetition cannot establish redundant control effectiveness.

This disposition table is outside the immutable baseline model. Under the user-authorized post-assessment decisions, all owning-file OD decisions are now closed at specification level and their SC rules are ACTIVE. Those statuses are normative only: no implementation exists. Follow the owning files for the current decision text. The original BLOCKED statuses in Appendix A remain historical baseline evidence; no row closes an implementation/effectiveness gap or assigns residual risk.

| T-006 | [SECURITY.md OD-004](SECURITY.md#od-004--api-transport-and-capacity) and OD-005; resource/execution contracts | Specified; unverified; operational measurements absent |

Product behavior belongs to [REQUIREMENTS.md](REQUIREMENTS.md); component/data-flow placement belongs to [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md); security decisions/rules belong to [SECURITY.md](SECURITY.md); rendering interactions belong to [DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md).

## 8. Requirement Traceability

The model preserves 98 exact requirements, authority `REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority`, edition `first release; no numbered edition/date supplied`, applicability `Trim Reaper CLI planned product`, evidence S07-E001. Exact texts remain in Appendix A. No legal obligation or implementation satisfaction is inferred. Explicit requirement-to-threat/control mapping records were not supplied; the locators attached to threats and controls remain evidence references, not an invented complete compliance matrix.

SC-001"}}]}],"requirements":[{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.1","source_id":"FR-7.1","text":"The system MUST accept a GitHub.com repository URL and an optional base branch through a non-interactive CLI.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.2","source_id":"FR-7.2","text":"The system MUST use the repository’s default branch when no base branch is supplied.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.3","source_id":"FR-7.3","text":"The system MUST reject local paths and repository hosts other than GitHub.com as project inputs.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.4","source_id":"FR-7.4","text":"The system MUST support public and private repositories accessible with the operator’s GitHub credentials.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.5","source_id":"FR-7.5","text":"The system MUST report missing repository permissions before it changes project files.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.6","source_id":"FR-7.6","text":"The system MUST perform project changes in an isolated working copy of the selected base revision.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.7","source_id":"FR-7.7","text":"The system MUST publish verified changes in one pull request per run against the selected base branch.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.8","source_id":"FR-7.8","text":"The system MUST NOT push changes directly to the base branch.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.9","source_id":"FR-7.9","text":"The system MUST NOT merge or approve its pull requests.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.10","source_id":"FR-7.10","text":"The system MUST report the pull request URL after successful publication.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.11","source_id":"FR-7.11","text":"The system MUST NOT create a pull request when no verified changes remain.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-7.12","source_id":"FR-7.12","text":"The system MUST report a publication failure without reporting the run as successful.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.1","source_id":"FR-8.1","text":"The system MUST support Java projects that use Maven or Gradle.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.2","source_id":"FR-8.2","text":"The system MUST support .NET projects that use NuGet through dotnet.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.3","source_id":"FR-8.3","text":"The system MUST support JavaScript and TypeScript projects that use npm, Yarn, or pnpm.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.4","source_id":"FR-8.4","text":"The system MUST evaluate each supported build root in a repository, including workspaces and repositories with multiple language families.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.5","source_id":"FR-8.5","text":"The system MUST use project-declared toolchain versions, wrappers, and package manager versions without upgrading them.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.6","source_id":"FR-8.6","text":"The system MUST require an explicit operator-supplied version when a required toolchain version is not declared.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.7","source_id":"FR-8.7","text":"The system MUST report unsupported build roots without changing their dependencies.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.8","source_id":"FR-8.8","text":"The system MUST require explicit installation, build, test, and representative runtime-flow commands for each build root selected for changes.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.9","source_id":"FR-8.9","text":"The system MUST accept those commands and their expected exit codes as CLI inputs, with zero as the default expected exit code.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.10","source_id":"FR-8.10","text":"The system MUST require operator-supplied acceptance assertions for each representative runtime flow.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.11","source_id":"FR-8.11","text":"The system MUST report missing commands, assertions, or required tools before dependency changes start.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-8.12","source_id":"FR-8.12","text":"The system MUST use the project’s existing packaging checks to verify production exclusions; when those checks are absent, it MUST leave production scope unchanged.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.1","source_id":"FR-1.1","text":"The system MUST record the current build results before dependency changes.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.2","source_id":"FR-1.2","text":"The system MUST record the current test results before dependency changes.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.3","source_id":"FR-1.3","text":"The system MUST check application code, dynamic loading, plugins, configuration, scripts, tests, and build and deployment tooling before it classifies a dependency as unused.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.4","source_id":"FR-1.4","text":"The system MUST investigate scanner results before it confirms that a dependency is unused.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.5","source_id":"FR-1.5","text":"The system MUST remove dependencies confirmed as unused.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.6","source_id":"FR-1.6","text":"The system MUST use the package manager to remove transitive dependencies that become unused after dependency changes.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.7","source_id":"FR-1.7","text":"The system MUST record the usage evidence from every area listed in FR-1.3 for each unused-dependency candidate.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.8","source_id":"FR-1.8","text":"The system MUST retain a candidate if dynamic loading, plugin registration, reflection, or external configuration leaves its use uncertain.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-1.9","source_id":"FR-1.9","text":"The system MUST confirm an unused-dependency removal only when the usage investigation finds no uses and the required checks pass without that dependency.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.1","source_id":"FR-2.1","text":"The system MUST identify dependency functions that the language standard library or supported platform APIs can replace directly.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.2","source_id":"FR-2.2","text":"The system MUST replace identified functions with those standard library functions or supported platform APIs.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.3","source_id":"FR-2.3","text":"The system MUST evaluate local replacement candidates for correctness and maintenance effort, rather than line count alone.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.5","source_id":"FR-2.5","text":"The system MUST provide focused tests for the required behavior and edge cases of each local replacement.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.7","source_id":"FR-2.7","text":"The system MUST remove a replaced dependency only after all its uses have been replaced.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.8","source_id":"FR-2.8","text":"The system MUST limit a local replacement to one module with no new dependency, no public API change, and no persistent state or new network or file access.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.9","source_id":"FR-2.9","text":"The system MUST retain libraries used for cryptography, authentication, protocol handling, general-purpose parsing, or concurrent coordination instead of providing local replacements for those functions.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.10","source_id":"FR-2.10","text":"The system MUST test each local replacement against the existing implementation for normal inputs, boundary inputs, invalid inputs, and error results; it MUST document any category that does not apply.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-2.11","source_id":"FR-2.11","text":"The system MUST defer replacements that exceed FR-2.8 or cannot satisfy FR-2.10.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.1","source_id":"FR-3.1","text":"The system MUST identify libraries that provide overlapping capabilities.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.2","source_id":"FR-3.2","text":"The system SHOULD replace overlapping uses with supported APIs from a library that the project already requires.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.3","source_id":"FR-3.3","text":"The system MUST remove libraries made redundant by those replacements.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.4","source_id":"FR-3.4","text":"The system MUST explain why each retained library is the better fit for the remaining requirements after consolidation.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.7","source_id":"FR-3.7","text":"The system MUST compare the resolved dependency graph before and after changes.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.8","source_id":"FR-3.8","text":"The system MUST NOT remove a transitive package that a retained dependency requires.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.9","source_id":"FR-3.9","text":"The system MUST limit consolidation to APIs supported by the retained library version already in the project.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.10","source_id":"FR-3.10","text":"The system MUST reject consolidation that changes public API types or adds dependencies between application modules.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.11","source_id":"FR-3.11","text":"The system MUST identify a large, lightly used dependency when it has at least ten unique transitive packages and the project uses at most three distinct public API entry points from it.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not implemented","evidence_ids":["S07-E001"],"source_artifact":"threat-model/07-documents.md"},{"id":"S07-R-FR-3.12","source_id":"FR-3.12","text":"The system MUST evaluate supported minimal packages, unused optional integrations, and existing alternatives for each candidate identified by FR-3.11.","authority":"REQUIREMENTS.md: sole product-behavior authority","edition":"first release; no numbered edition/date supplied","applicability":"Trim Reaper CLI planned product","status":"normative requirement; not T-001: Project execution reaches operator authority T-002: Untrusted observations substitute for policy T-003: Sensitive evidence crosses disclosure boundaries T-004: Rendered evidence spoofs trusted report status T-005: Publication is not bound to verified state T-006: Unbounded external work prevents completion T-007: Untrusted execution compromises run environment T-008: Evidence-to-publication integrity failure T-009: Private evidence disclosed through required outputs T-010: Conditional personal-data disclosure in evidence T-011: Run project-controlled operations outside the intended execution boundary T-012: Publish changes not covered by authoritative verification T-013: Misrepresent results through rendered untrusted content T-014: Untrusted output is interpreted as presentation instructions T-015: Restoration does not restore all affected roots T-016: Uncertain dynamic usage is classified unused T-017: Project execution exceeds intended filesystem boundary T-018: Recorded PASS is stale relative to final revision T-019: Publication retry duplicates PR or reports false success T-020: Unavailable or malformed analysis is treated as usable evidence

### Appendix F. Glossary

- **Conditional design threat:** Supported exposure whose success depends on an unverified condition or control failure.
- **Evidence gap:** Missing information; not proof of a defect.
- **ACTIVE:** Normative rule status, not implementation proof.
- **BLOCKED:** Baseline rule awaits an unresolved decision.
- **Unrated:** No supported rating method/input is supplied.
- **Trust boundary:** Transition between different authorities or trust assumptions.
- **Figure brief:** Instructions and accessible text for a later renderer, not a rendered image.
